# Public content trace — Step 13

Every one of the **662 data rows** in CONTENT_MATRIX is listed below, including the original 347 baseline rows, later exact editor reconciliation and Step 13 additions. Duplicate rows intentionally retain their original lineage; this is a per-row audit, not a second content catalog. Canonical schema names supersede earlier proposal names. Source values remain only in initialization snapshots/database, never in this runtime mapping.

The trace was assembled from the entire matrix, actual code-defined registry/editor assignments, actual initialized database keys, server FormRequests/managers and public templates/services. All referenced model paths/consumers were inspected. Read-only/technical media, private staff/security fields and historical unused copy are explicitly closed boundaries. No unresolved public content implementation remains.

Verification: 97 named fields initialized and assigned exactly once; 95 runtime consumers plus blog.blogdesc/contact.mode_apply intentionally unused. Protected editor → database → public HTML mutation tests cover regular localized controls and both locales; focused existing suites cover request feedback, every collection, links, paragraph independence, visibility/publication and uploads. Full 126-test suite passed; real localhost editor saves independently verified durable en/bn/retention/fallback on both the working app and release without frontend/seed input. Source/build scans found zero catalogs/demo copy/runtime source reads. See IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS for exact counts/boundaries and ADMIN_CONTENT_GUIDE for staff instructions.
